# Turns the stored moments of a run back into text for the conclusion screen.
# underdeep_chronicle_effect = { LINE = x } stores flag:ud_chr_x in the next free slot (underdeep_chron_1 to _10).
# Each slot below lists every line, so a new chronicle line needs an entry in all ten slots,
# plus underdeep_chr_x in localization/english/tfw_underdeep_l_english.yml.

# What a character calls Morgoth.
# Use in text as [ROOT.Char.Custom('underdeep_enemy_name')]
underdeep_enemy_name = {
	type = character

	text = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_orc = yes
				is_troll = yes
			}
			NOT = { faith_is_good = yes }
		}
		localization_key = underdeep_enemy_first_master
	}

	text = {
		trigger = { faith_is_evil = yes }
		localization_key = underdeep_enemy_lord_of_the_dark
	}

	text = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_elf = yes
				is_dwarf = yes
			}
		}
		localization_key = underdeep_enemy_morgoth
	}

	text = {
		trigger = { always = yes }
		localization_key = underdeep_enemy_dark_lord_of_old
	}
}


# What a door or tunnel looks like at a junction. The first line whose trigger is true is shown.
# "Read" versions are for a host whose skill reads that door: the clue of the room it leads to (see tfw_tfw_underdeep_effects.txt),
# or one of four lines for the room kind. Otherwise one of six looks, picked by underdeep_setup_junction_effect.

UnderdeepDoorTreasureRead = {
	type = character
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1102 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1102 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1107 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1107 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1110 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1110 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1113 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1113 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1118 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1118 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1123 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1123 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1126 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1126 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1130 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1130 }
	text = { trigger = { underdeep_room_picked = { KIND = treasure ID = 1132 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_clue_1132 }
	text = { trigger = { involved_activity = { var:underdeep_hint_treasure = 1 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_treasure_learned }
	text = { trigger = { involved_activity = { var:underdeep_hint_treasure = 2 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_treasure_learned_2 }
	text = { trigger = { involved_activity = { var:underdeep_hint_treasure = 3 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_treasure_learned_3 }
	text = { trigger = { involved_activity = { var:underdeep_hint_treasure = 4 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_treasure_learned_4 }

	text = { trigger = { involved_activity = { var:underdeep_hint_treasure = 6 } } localization_key = underdeep_door_treasure_learned_2 }
	text = { fallback = yes localization_key = underdeep_door_treasure_learned_2 }
}
